                            <h1 class="mt-4 font-display text-base uppercase tracking-wider text-ink sm:text-xl">How it works</h1>
                            <div class="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-muted sm:text-sm">Stonks Exchange is a launchpad whose quote asset is a share, not a coin.</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </header>
                <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b-2 border-line bg-panel-lit px-3 py-2">
                        <h2 class="font-display text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
                            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                <svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" width="14" height="14" fill="currentColor" shape-rendering="crispEdges" class="text-base-blue-lit" aria-hidden="true">
                                    <rect x="4" y="0" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="3" y="1" width="3" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="2" y="2" width="5" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="1" y="3" width="7" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="3" y="4" width="3" height="5"></rect>
                                </svg>
                                The launch
                            </span>
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    <div class="p-3">
                        <ol class="space-y-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">
                            <li>
                                <span class="font-display text-[9px] uppercase text-base-blue-lit">01 — Deploy</span>
                                <p class="mt-1">A fixed-supply ERC-20 is deployed: one billion tokens, 18 decimals, no mint, no pause, no blacklist, no owner. Deliberately the opposite of the equity it trades against — the quote side is issuer-controlled by design, the coin side is ungoverned by construction.</p>
                            </li>
                            <li>
                                <span class="font-display text-[9px] uppercase text-base-blue-lit">02 — Open</span>
                                <p class="mt-1">
                                    The whole supply is placed as ONE single-sided Uniswap V3 position on the 1% tier, spanning from the launch tick to the edge of the range. The opening tick is derived from the stock &#x27;s <span class="text-ink">Chainlink price at that moment</span>
                                    , targeting the same ~$4,000 opening cap for every coin — which is what makes two coins quoted in different stocks comparable at all. The pool opens holding
                                    <!-- -->
                                    <span class="text-ink">100% coin and 0% stock</span>
                                    . Buyers walk the price up as they buy — real price discovery, no bonding curve.
                                </p>
                            </li>
                            <li>
                                <span class="font-display text-[9px] uppercase text-base-blue-lit">03 — Lock</span>
                                <p class="mt-1">
                                    The LP position NFT goes straight to a fee locker whose code has no
                                    <!-- -->
                                    <code>decreaseLiquidity</code>
                                    , no <code>burn</code>
                                    , no NFT transfer and no approval path, and whose upgrade function reverts unconditionally for everyone — the owner included. The principal cannot leave. Only swap fees can ever be collected.
                                </p>
                            </li>
                        </ol>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel p-4">
                        <h3 class="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-base-blue-lit">
                            <svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" width="13" height="13" fill="currentColor" shape-rendering="crispEdges" aria-hidden="true">
                                <rect x="1" y="1" width="2" height="2"></rect>
                                <rect x="6" y="6" width="2" height="2"></rect>
                                <rect x="6" y="1" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="5" y="2" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="4" y="3" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="4" y="4" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="3" y="5" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="2" y="6" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                            </svg>
                            Fees on every trade
                        </h3>
                        <p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">
                            Each 1% swap fee splits two ways: <span class="text-ink">0.7% to the creator</span>
                            <!-- -->
                            and 0.3% to the platform. Anyone can crank collection permissionlessly. Creators choose where their share lands — at launch, or any time after — without ever handing over control of the coin. The platform &#x27;s share funds a
                            <!-- -->
                            <a class="text-base-blue-lit underline" href="/stats">$STONKEX buyback-and-burn</a>
                            : 80% of it buys $STONKEX and destroys it, 20% is operator revenue.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel p-4">
                        <h3 class="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-base-blue-lit">
                            <svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" width="13" height="13" fill="currentColor" shape-rendering="crispEdges" aria-hidden="true">
                                <rect x="4" y="0" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="2" y="1" width="2" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="5" y="1" width="2" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="1" y="2" width="7" height="1"></rect>
                                <rect x="4" y="2" width="1" height="6"></rect>
                                <rect x="2" y="3" width="5" height="5"></rect>
                            </svg>
                            Launch before the market
                        </h3>
                        <p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">
                            Because launch liquidity is entirely coin, a coin can be launched against an equity that has no circulating supply and no pool of its own. Most of Base &#x27;s equities are still at zero supply — see the
                            <!-- -->
                            <a class="text-base-blue-lit underline" href="/stocks">stocks page</a>
                            .
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b-2 border-line bg-panel-lit px-3 py-2">
                        <h2 class="font-display text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
                            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                <span class="grid size-5 shrink-0 place-items-center bg-[#05070f] p-0.5">
                                    <img src="/img/fomo.png" alt="" class="size-3.5 object-contain"/>
                                </span>
                                FOMO airdrops
                            </span>
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    <div class="p-3">
                        <div class="space-y-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">
                            <p>
                                A launch can hand a slice of its supply — <span class="text-ink">1% to 10%</span>
                                — to the top traders of the
                                <!-- -->
                                <a class="text-base-blue-lit underline" href="/kols">FOMO leaderboard</a>
                                , chosen right in the launch form: the top 50, 25, 10, or a hand-picked set, split equally.
                            </p>
                            <p>
                                The supply is <span class="text-ink">bought from the coin &#x27;s own pool</span>
                                by the dev buy, not minted. Every airdropped token is one that first deepened the pool, so the “100% in the pool” guarantee holds — nothing is quietly set aside for insiders, and the airdrop is real spend, not dilution.
                            </p>
                            <p>
                                What each recipient does next is graded <span class="text-ink">from the chain, not by us</span>
                                : sold within three days is red, held three days orange, held a week green, and a wallet that buys more earns a diamond. The record is algorithmic — nobody, us included, can edit it — and the recipients plus the list version are recorded on-chain per batch, so a batch that pays wallets off the list is visible as exactly that.
                            </p>
                            <p>
                                Coins that complete an airdrop wear a
                                <!-- -->
                                <span class="inline-flex translate-y-0.5 items-center gap-1 border border-gold px-1 py-px align-middle font-display text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider text-gold">
                                    <span class="grid size-3 shrink-0 place-items-center bg-[#05070f] p-0.5">
                                        <img src="/img/fomo.png" alt="" class="size-2.5 object-contain"/>
                                    </span>
                                    airdrop
                                </span>
                                <!-- -->
                                tag on the board; the traders build a public hold-record on the leaderboard. Real supporters get rewarded, dumpers get flagged.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b-2 border-line bg-panel-lit px-3 py-2">
                        <h2 class="font-display text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
                            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                <svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" width="14" height="14" fill="currentColor" shape-rendering="crispEdges" class="text-base-blue-lit" aria-hidden="true">
                                    <rect x="3" y="1" width="3" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="3" y="2" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="5" y="2" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                                    <rect x="2" y="3" width="5" height="5"></rect>
                                </svg>
                                The contracts
                            </span>
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    <div class="p-3 p-0">
                        <ul class="divide-y-2 divide-line">
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkLauncher2</span>
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap border px-1 py-0.5 font-display text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider sm:px-1.5 border-base-blue text-base-blue-lit">upgrades frozen</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">Deploys the token, opens its Uniswap V3 pool at a price derived from the stock’s own Chainlink feed in the launch transaction, mints the whole supply as one single-sided position, and hands the LP NFT to the locker. Which locker it hands to is fixed at deployment — there is no setter for it. A dev buy can be funded in the stock, or in plain ETH routed through an allowlisted aggregator.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0x4714f6EC81639Ca59EEBE634490a4d8671DCe7B4" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0x4714f6EC81639Ca59EEBE634490a4d8671DCe7B4</a>
                            </li>
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkFeeLocker2</span>
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap border px-1 py-0.5 font-display text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider sm:px-1.5 border-base-blue text-base-blue-lit">no withdraw path</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">Holds every launch’s LP NFT forever. Its only position-touching calls are collect (fees) and increaseLiquidity (additive). No decreaseLiquidity, no burn, no NFT transfer, no approval — and any upgrade attempt reverts, for everyone.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0x71D1D363176723f85d98B8B430DF33cde89f0A7f" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0x71D1D363176723f85d98B8B430DF33cde89f0A7f</a>
                            </li>
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkQuoteRegistry2</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">Which assets a token may be paired with, and the opening price for each — kept honest by Chainlink. Every launch re-reads the stock’s feed and re-anchors the opening tick when the market has moved more than ~4%; if the feed is stale or down, the last anchor carries the launch instead of blocking it. A stock listed by Base later is added here — no redeploy, and this site picks it up on its own.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0x4db9F13325A83662cf992184bc070755a212e95B" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0x4db9F13325A83662cf992184bc070755a212e95B</a>
                            </li>
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkTradeRouter3</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">Buys and sells against a token’s own pool, with a slippage floor and a deadline on every call. ETH in or out converts through an owner-allowlisted aggregator (0x, Uniswap, Aerodrome) — wherever the stock’s liquidity ends up living, the route follows it, and a sell to ETH converts 100% of the proceeds with zero stock left as dust. Custodies nothing between transactions.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0x01F178473DcaC0CE4b2B2111BecFB074b586dd12" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0x01F178473DcaC0CE4b2B2111BecFB074b586dd12</a>
                            </li>
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkQuoter</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">Simulates the exact swap the router will perform and reverts with the answer, so the amount you are shown is the amount you get.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0x2826DF040b68F528f5DEF00A5727e14691B755b4" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0x2826DF040b68F528f5DEF00A5727e14691B755b4</a>
                            </li>
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkDisperse</span>
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap border px-1 py-0.5 font-display text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider sm:px-1.5 border-base-blue text-base-blue-lit">ownerless</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">The KOL-airdrop batch pusher: sends a slice of supply to many wallets in one transaction, straight from the sender, keeping nothing. No owner, no fees, no custody — and the event it emits names which FOMO list version a batch paid, so an indexer can verify the recipients match it.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0x3e3F3A9f15614FA40244219F025C88602db58e1c" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0x3e3F3A9f15614FA40244219F025C88602db58e1c</a>
                            </li>
                            <li class="px-3 py-3">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="font-display text-[10px] text-ink">StonkFeeSplitter</span>
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap border px-1 py-0.5 font-display text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider sm:px-1.5 border-base-blue text-base-blue-lit">buyback &amp; burn</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">Where the platform’s 0.3% cut lands. A keeper takes 20% as operator revenue and spends the other 80% buying $STONKEX on the market and burning it — a continuous buyback-and-burn funded by every coin’s trades. Launchpad coins it receives are held, never sold. See the burn dashboard for the running total.</p>
                                <a href="https://basescan.org/address/0xfBC9eE130f1CFeeb192b18CF1202865d757FA680" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="mt-1.5 inline-block break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-muted underline decoration-line-lit underline-offset-2 hover:text-base-blue-lit">0xfBC9eE130f1CFeeb192b18CF1202865d757FA680</a>
                            </li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel p-4">
                    <h3 class="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-base-blue-lit">
                        <svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" width="13" height="13" fill="currentColor" shape-rendering="crispEdges" aria-hidden="true">
                            <rect x="1" y="0" width="7" height="1"></rect>
                            <rect x="1" y="1" width="7" height="3"></rect>
                            <rect x="2" y="4" width="5" height="1"></rect>
                            <rect x="3" y="5" width="3" height="1"></rect>
                            <rect x="4" y="6" width="1" height="1"></rect>
                        </svg>
                        What the operator can and cannot do
                    </h3>
                    <p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-dim">
                        The owner can list stocks and their price feeds, override an opening anchor, and — each behind a <span class="text-ink">24-hour announced on-chain delay</span>
                        — change the fee split within hard caps that always leave the creator at least 20%, or reassign an abandoned token &#x27;s creator. The owner <span class="text-ink">cannot</span>
                        touch any LP position or locked liquidity, upgrade either contract, mint or pause a launched token, or change which locker receives a launch. Those are absent from the code, not promised.
                    </p>
                </div>
                <div class="vox-sheen relative border-2 shadow-pixel border-line bg-panel">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b-2 border-line bg-panel-lit px-3 py-2">
                        <h2 class="font-display text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
                            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                <svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" width="14" height="14" fill="currentColor" shape-rendering="crispEdges" class="text-base-blue-lit" aria-hidden="true">
                                    <rect x="0" y="5" width="2" height="4"></rect>
                                    <rect x="3" y="3" width="2" height="6"></rect>
                                    <rect x="6" y="1" width="2" height="8"></rect>
                                </svg>
                                Why stock splits are safe here
                            </span>
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    <div class="p-3">
                        <div class="space-y-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">
                            <p>
                                This is the question that decides whether the whole idea works. Base represents a corporate action — a split, a reinvested dividend — by moving a B20 Asset &#x27;s
                                <!-- -->
                                <span class="text-ink">multiplier</span>
                                . If that were a rebase, a Uniswap V3 pool holding the equity would silently drift out of step with its own accounting, and every launch would need a wrapper token.
                            </p>
                            <p>
                                It is not a rebase. Under ERC-8056 the multiplier is cosmetic: it rescales the balance a holder is <em>shown</em>
                                without minting, transferring, or rewriting any raw balance, and B20 tells integrators to treat raw amounts as canonical. The ERC-20 surface —
                                <!-- -->
                                <code>balanceOf</code>
                                , <code>transfer</code>
                                , <code>totalSupply</code>
                                — is non-rebasing.
                            </p>
                            <p>So a 2:1 split doubles the share count on your screen and moves nothing in the pool: same raw reserves, same price, same liquidity, same swap output. That is asserted directly in the test suite, by executing a real split against a real pool.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <!--$-->
